::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: {#api-reference .section}
[]{#module-flogin}

# API Reference[¶](#api-reference "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

The following section outlines the API of flogin.

::: {#version-related-info .section}
## Version Related Info[¶](#version-related-info "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

There are two main ways to query version information about the library.
For guarantees, check [[Version Guarantees]{.std
.std-ref}](version_guarantees.html#version-guarantees){.reference
.internal}.

[[flogin.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[version_info]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.version_info "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A named tuple that is similar to [[`sys.version_info`{.xref .py
  .py-obj .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/sys.html#sys.version_info "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
  .external}.

  Just like [[`sys.version_info`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/sys.html#sys.version_info "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
  .external} the valid values for [`releaselevel`{.docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre} are 'alpha', 'beta', 'candidate' and 'final'.

<!-- -->

[[flogin.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[\_\_version\_\_]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.__version__ "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A string representation of the version. e.g. [`'1.0.0rc1'`{.docutils
  .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}. This is based off of []{#index-0
  .target}[**PEP 440**](https://peps.python.org/pep-0440/){.pep
  .reference .external}.
:::

::: {#types .section}
## Types[¶](#types "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

[[[type]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[Jsonable]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.Jsonable "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This is a type used to represent anything json serializable.

  It is literally defined as:

  :::: {.highlight-py3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      str | int | float | None | bool | list["Jsonable"] | dict[str, "Jsonable"]
  :::
  ::::

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.plugin.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[Plugin]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[\[]{.sig-paren}*[[SettingsT]{.pre}]{.n}*[\]]{.sig-paren}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[\*\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[options]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/plugin.html#Plugin){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.plugin.Plugin "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This class represents your plugin.

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : - **settings_no_update** (Optional\[[[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- Whether or not to let flow update flogin's
      version of the settings. This can be useful when using a custom
      settings menu. Defaults to [`False`{.docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}

    - **ignore_cancellation_requests** (Optional\[[[`bool`{.xref .py
      .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- Whether or not to ignore cancellation requests
      sent from flow. Defaults to [`False`{.docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}

    - **disable_log_override_files** (Optional\[[[`bool`{.xref .py
      .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- Whether or not to disable the log override files.
      Defaults to [`False`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}. See
      [[Log Override Files]{.doc}](log-override-files.html){.reference
      .internal} for more information on this.

  [[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[event]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/plugin.html#Plugin.event){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.event "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A decorator that registers an event to listen for. This decorator
    can be used with a plugin instance or as a classmethod.

    All events must be a [[coroutine]{.xref .std
    .std-ref}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external}.

    ::: versionchanged
    [Changed in version 1.1.0: ]{.versionmodified .changed}The decorator
    can now be used as a classmethod
    :::

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    See the [[event reference]{.std
    .std-ref}](events.html#events){.reference .internal} to see what
    valid events there are.
    :::

    Example

    With a plugin instance:

    :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        from flogin.utils import print

        @plugin.event
        async def on_initialization():
            print('Ready!')
    :::
    ::::

    As a classmethod:

    :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        from flogin.utils import print

        class MyPlugin(Plugin):
            @Plugin.event
            async def on_initialization(self):
                print('Ready!')
    :::
    ::::

  [[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[search]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[condition]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[text]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[pattern]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[keyword]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[allowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[disallowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/plugin.html#Plugin.search){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.search "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A decorator that registers a search handler.

    All search handlers must be a [[coroutine]{.xref .std
    .std-ref}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external}. See the [[search handler section]{.std
    .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#search-handlers){.reference
    .internal} for more information about using search handlers.

    ::: versionchanged
    [Changed in version 2.0.0: ]{.versionmodified .changed}The search
    decorator can now be used as a classmethod
    :::

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    This decorator can also be used as a classmethod
    :::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : - **condition** (Optional\[[[condition]{.std
        .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#condition-example){.reference
        .internal}\]) -- The condition to determine which queries this
        handler should run on. If given, this should be the only
        argument given.

      - **text** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- A kwarg to quickly add a
        [[`PlainTextCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.PlainTextCondition "flogin.conditions.PlainTextCondition"){.reference
        .internal}. If given, this should be the only argument given.

      - **pattern** (Optional\[[[`re.Pattern`{.xref .py .py-class
        .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/re.html#re.Pattern "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external} \| [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- A kwarg to quickly add a
        [[`RegexCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.RegexCondition "flogin.conditions.RegexCondition"){.reference
        .internal}. If given, this should be the only argument given.

      - **keyword** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- A kwarg to quickly set the condition to a
        [[`KeywordCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition"){.reference
        .internal} condition with the [`keyword`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre} kwarg being the only allowed keyword.

      - **allowed_keywords** (Optional\[Iterable\[[[`str`{.xref .py
        .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]\]) -- A kwarg to quickly set the condition to a
        [[`KeywordCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition"){.reference
        .internal} condition with the kwarg being the list of allowed
        keywords.

      - **disallowed_keywords** (Optional\[Iterable\[[[`str`{.xref .py
        .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]\]) -- A kwarg to quickly set the condition to a
        [[`KeywordCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition"){.reference
        .internal} condition with the kwarg being the list of disallowed
        keywords.

    Example

    With an instance:

    :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        @plugin.search()
        async def example_search_handler(data: Query):
            return "This is a result!"
    :::
    ::::

    As a classmethod:

    :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        class MyPlugin(Plugin):
            @Plugin.search()
            async def example_search_handler(self, data: Query):
                return "This is a result!"
    :::
    ::::

    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Applies updates to the query with flow, and to this object.

    This method provides quick acess to
    [[`flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI.change_query()`{.xref .py
    .py-func .docutils .literal

    .internal}

        .external} \| [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}\])
        --

        The text that will be used with the query.

        ::: versionchanged
        [Changed in version 2.0.0: ]{.versionmodified
        .changed}[`text`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} can now

        :::

      - **keyword** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal

        .external} \| [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}\])
        -- The keyword that will be used with the query. Defaults to the
        pre-existing value of [[`Query.keyword`{.xref .py .py-attr
        .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.query.Query.keyword "flogin.query.Query.keyword"){.reference
        .internal}. Set this to [`None`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre} or [`*`{.docutils .literal

      - **requery** (Optional\[[[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- Whether or not to re-send a query request in

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[update_results]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[results]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/query.html#Query.update_results){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.query.Query.update_results "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Tells flow to change the results shown to the user, using the query
    from this query object.

    This method provides quick acess to
    [[`flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI.update_results()`{.xref .py
    .py-func .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI.update_results "flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI.update_results"){.reference
    .internal}. Because of that, this method will only take affect if
    the user has not changed the query.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : **results** (list\[[[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal}\]) -- The new results

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : [**JsonRPCException**](#flogin.jsonrpc.errors.JsonRPCException "flogin.jsonrpc.errors.JsonRPCException"){.reference
      .internal} -- This is raised when an error happens with the
      JsonRPC pipe while attempting to call this API method.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.jsonrpc.results.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[Result]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[\[]{.sig-paren}*[[PluginT]{.pre}]{.n}*[\]]{.sig-paren}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[title]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[sub]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[icon]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[title_highlight_data]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[title_tooltip]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[sub_tooltip]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[copy_text]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[score]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[auto_complete_text]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[preview]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[progress_bar]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[rounded_icon]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[glyph]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/jsonrpc/results.html#Result){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This represents a result that would be returned as a result for a
  query or context menu.

  For simple useage: create instances of this class as-is.

  For advanced useage (handling clicks and custom context menus), it is
  recommended to subclass the result object to create your own subclass.

  ::::: {#subclassing .section}
  #### Subclassing[¶](#subclassing "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

  Subclassing lets you override the following methods:
  [[`callback()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.callback "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.callback"){.reference
  .internal} and [[`context_menu()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils
  .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.context_menu "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.context_menu"){.reference
  .internal}. It also lets you create "universal" result properties (eg:
  same icon). Example:

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      class MyResult(Result):
          def __init__(self, title: str) -> None:
              super().__init__(self, title, icon="Images/app.png")

          async def callback(self):
              # handle what happens when the result gets clicked

          async def context_menu(self):
              # add context menu options to this result's context menu
  :::
  ::::

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[callback]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/jsonrpc/results.html#Result.callback){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.callback "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Override this function to add a callback behavior to your result.
    This method will run when the user clicks on your result.

    ::: versionchanged
    [Changed in version 2.0.0: ]{.versionmodified .changed}A result
    callback can not return [[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external} or [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}
    :::

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`ExecuteResponse`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ExecuteResponse "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ExecuteResponse"){.reference
      .internal} \| [[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} \| [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} -- A
      response to flow determining whether or not to hide flow's menu,
      or a bool that will be turned into a response. [`None`{.docutils
      .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} will be converted into
      [`True`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} to align with the
      default value for [[`hide`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ExecuteResponse.hide "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ExecuteResponse.hide"){.reference
      .internal}

  <!-- -->

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[context_menu]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/jsonrpc/results.html#Result.context_menu){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.context_menu "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Override this function to add a context menu behavior to your
    result. This method will run when the user gets the context menu to
    your result.

    This method can return/yield almost anything, and flogin will
    convert it into a list of [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
    .internal} objects before sending it to flow.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : list\[[[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal}\] \| [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A list of results, an results, or
      something that can be converted into a list of results.

    Yields[:]{.colon}

    : [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A result object or something that can
      be converted into a result object.

  <!-- -->

  [[classmethod]{.pre} ]{.property}[[create_with_partial]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[partial_callback]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[\*\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[kwargs]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/jsonrpc/results.html#Result.create_with_partial){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.create_with_partial "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A quick and easy way to create a result with a callback without
    subclassing.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    This is meant to be used with [[`FlowLauncherAPI`{.xref .py
    .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI "flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI"){.reference
    .internal} methods
    :::

    Example

    :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        result = Result.create_with_partial(
            functools.partial(
                plugin.api.show_notification,
                "notification title",
                "notification content"
            ),
            title="Result title",
            sub="Result subtitle"
        )
    :::
    ::::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : - **partial_callback** (partial [[coroutine]{.xref .std
        .std-ref}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The callback wrapped in
        [[`functools.partial`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functools.html#functools.partial "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}

      - **kwargs** (See allowed kwargs here: [[`Result`{.xref .py
        .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
        .internal}) -- The args that will be passed to the
        [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
        .internal} constructor

  <!-- -->

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[on_context_menu_error]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[error]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/jsonrpc/results.html#Result.on_context_menu_error){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result.on_context_menu_error "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Override this function to add an error response behavior to this
    result's context menu callback.

    If the error was handled:

    : You can return/yield almost anything, and flogin will convert it
      into a list of [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} objects before sending it to flow.

    If the error was not handled:

    : Return a [[`ErrorResponse`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse"){.reference
      .internal} object

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : **error** ([[`Exception`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#Exception "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}) -- The error that occured

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`ErrorResponse`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse"){.reference
      .internal} \| list\[[[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal}\] \| [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A list of results, an results, or

      be converted into a result object.

::: {.py-attribute-table move-to-id="flogin.jsonrpc.results.ResultConstructorKwargs"}
:::

[[[typeddict]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.jsonrpc.results.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[ResultConstructorKwargs]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/jsonrpc/results.html#ResultConstructorKwargs){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.ResultConstructorKwargs "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This represents the possible kwargs that can be passed to
  [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
  .internal}.

  This can be useful when overriding [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class
  .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
  .internal} to create a basic implementation of something for your
  project, but still want the ability to pass kwargs with proper typing.

  ::: {.admonition .note}
  Note

  See [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
  .internal} for more information about each key and value.
  :::

  Example

  This is an example of how you might use this to create a url result

  :::: {.highlight-py3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      from typing import Unpack
      from flogin import Result, ResultConstructorKwargs

      class MyRes(Result):
          def __init__(self, url: str, **kwargs: Unpack[ResultConstructorKwargs]) -> None:
              self.url = url
              super().__init__(**kwargs)

          async def callback(self):
              await self.plugin.api.open_url(self.url)
  :::
  ::::

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.search_handler.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[SearchHandler]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[\[]{.sig-paren}*[[PluginT]{.pre}]{.n}*[\]]{.sig-paren}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[condition]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[text]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[pattern]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[keyword]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[allowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[disallowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/search_handler.html#SearchHandler){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This represents a search handler.

  When creating this on your own, the
  [[`register_search_handler()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.register_search_handler "flogin.plugin.Plugin.register_search_handler"){.reference
  .internal} method can be used to register it.

  See the [[search handler section]{.std
  .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#search-handlers){.reference .internal}
  for more information about using search handlers.

  There is a provided decorator to easily create search handlers:
  [[`search()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.search "flogin.plugin.Plugin.search"){.reference
  .internal}

  The keywords in the constructor can also be passed into the subclassed
  init, like so:

  :::: {.highlight-default .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      class MyHandler(SearchHandler, text="text"):
          ...

      # is equal to

      class MyHandler(SearchHandler):
          def __init__(self):
              super().__init__(text="text")
  :::
  ::::

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : - **condition** (Optional\[[[condition]{.std
      .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#condition-example){.reference
      .internal}\]) -- The condition to determine which queries this
      handler should run on. If given, this should be the only argument
      given.

    - **text** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- A kwarg to quickly add a
      [[`PlainTextCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.PlainTextCondition "flogin.conditions.PlainTextCondition"){.reference
      .internal}. If given, this should be the only argument given.

    - **pattern** (Optional\[[[`re.Pattern`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/re.html#re.Pattern "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} \| [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- A kwarg to quickly add a [[`RegexCondition`{.xref
      .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.RegexCondition "flogin.conditions.RegexCondition"){.reference
      .internal}. If given, this should be the only argument given.

    - **keyword** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- A kwarg to quickly set the condition to a
      [[`KeywordCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition"){.reference
      .internal} condition with the [`keyword`{.docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre} kwarg being the only allowed keyword.

    - **allowed_keywords** (Optional\[Iterable\[[[`str`{.xref .py
      .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]\]) -- A kwarg to quickly set the condition to a
      [[`KeywordCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition"){.reference
      .internal} condition with the kwarg being the list of allowed
      keywords.

    - **disallowed_keywords** (Optional\[Iterable\[[[`str`{.xref .py
      .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]\]) -- A kwarg to quickly set the condition to a
      [[`KeywordCondition`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition"){.reference
      .internal} condition with the kwarg being the list of disallowed
      keywords.

  [[plugin]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.plugin "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Your plugin instance. This is filled before [[`callback()`{.xref .py
    .py-func .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.callback "flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.callback"){.reference
    .internal} is triggered.

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : [[`Plugin`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin "flogin.plugin.Plugin"){.reference
      .internal} \| None

  [[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[error]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/search_handler.html#SearchHandler.error){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.error "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A decorator that registers a error handler for this search handler.

    For more information see [[`on_error`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.on_error "flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.on_error"){.reference
    .internal}

    Example

    :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        @plugin.search()
        async def my_hander(query):
            ..

        @my_handler.error
        async def my_error_handler(query, error):
            ...
    :::
    ::::

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[callback]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[query]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/search_handler.html#SearchHandler.callback){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.callback "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Override this function to add the search handler behavior you want
    for the set condition.

    This method can return/yield almost anything, and flogin will
    convert it into a list of [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
    .internal} objects before sending it to flow.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : list\[[[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal}\] \| [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A list of results, an results, or
      something that can be converted into a list of results.

    Yields[:]{.colon}

    : [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A result object or something that can
      be converted into a result object.

  [[condition]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[query]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/search_handler.html#SearchHandler.condition){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.condition "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A function that determines whether or not to fire off this search
    handler for a given query

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : **query** ([[`Query`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.query.Query "flogin.query.Query"){.reference

      .external} -- Whether or not to fire off this handler for the

  [[[property]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[name]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.name "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : The name of the search handler's callback

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[on_error]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[query]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[error]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/search_handler.html#SearchHandler.on_error){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.search_handler.SearchHandler.on_error "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Override this function to add an error response behavior to this
    handler's callback.

    If the error was handled:

    : You can return/yield almost anything, and flogin will convert it
      into a list of [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} objects before sending it to flow.

    If the error was not handled:

    : Return a [[`ErrorResponse`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse"){.reference
      .internal} object

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : - **query** ([[`Query`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.query.Query "flogin.query.Query"){.reference
        .internal}) -- The query that was being handled when the error
        occured.

      - **error** ([[`Exception`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#Exception "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The error that occured

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`ErrorResponse`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.ErrorResponse"){.reference
      .internal} \| list\[[[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal}\] \| [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A list of results, an results, or
      something that can be converted into a list of results.

    Yields[:]{.colon}

    : [[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
      .internal} \| str \| Any -- A result object or something that can
      be converted into a result object.

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.conditions.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[PlainTextCondition]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[text]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/conditions.html#PlainTextCondition){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.conditions.PlainTextCondition "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A builtin search condition to check plain text.

  This condition will only run if the query's text is the same as the
  text given to this condition. See the [[search handler section]{.std
  .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#search-handlers){.reference .internal}
  for more information about using search handlers and conditions.

  [[text]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.conditions.PlainTextCondition.text "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : The text to compare the query to

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}

Attributes

::::

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.conditions.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[RegexCondition]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[pattern]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/conditions.html#RegexCondition){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.conditions.RegexCondition "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A builtin search condition to check a regex pattern.

  This condition will only run if the query's text is a match to the
  regex pattern given to this condition. See the [[search handler
  section]{.std
  .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#search-handlers){.reference .internal}
  for more information about using search handlers and conditions.

  This condition will set the query's [[`condition_data`{.xref .py
  .py-attr .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.query.Query.condition_data "flogin.query.Query.condition_data"){.reference
  .internal} attribute to the [[`re.Match`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
  .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/re.html#re.Match "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
  .external} object.

  [[pattern]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.conditions.RegexCondition.pattern "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : The pattern to check the queries against.

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : [[`re.Pattern`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/re.html#re.Pattern "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.conditions.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[KeywordCondition]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[allowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[disallowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/conditions.html#KeywordCondition){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A builtin search condition to check what keyword was used with the
  query.

  If the [[`allowed_keywords`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition.allowed_keywords "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition.allowed_keywords"){.reference
  .internal} attribute is given, the handler will only run if the
  query's keyword is in the list of allowed keywords. If the
  [[`disallowed_keywords`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition.disallowed_keywords "flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition.disallowed_keywords"){.reference
  .internal} attribute is given, the handler will only run if the
  query's keyword is not in the list of allowed keywords.

  See the [[search handler section]{.std
  .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#search-handlers){.reference .internal}
  for more information about using search handlers and conditions.

  [[allowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition.allowed_keywords "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : The allowed keywords

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : Optional\[Iterable\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]\]

  [[disallowed_keywords]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.conditions.KeywordCondition.disallowed_keywords "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : The disallowed keywords

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : Optional\[Iterable\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]\]

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.conditions.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[AllCondition]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[conditions]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/conditions.html#AllCondition){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.conditions.AllCondition "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This builtin search condition acts similiarly to the builtin
  [`all`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} function. It only
  returns [`True`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} if all of the
  given conditions also return [`True`{.docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}.

  This condition will set [[`flogin.query.Query.condition_data`{.xref
  .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.query.Query.condition_data "flogin.query.Query.condition_data"){.reference
  .internal} to a dictionary containing the conditions, where the keys
  are the conditions, and the values are the condition data that they
  gave.

  [[conditions]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.conditions.AllCondition.conditions "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A list that contains all the conditions that should be used with
    this condition.

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : list\[[[condition]{.std
      .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#condition-example){.reference
      .internal}\]

:::: {.py-attribute-table move-to-id="flogin.conditions.AnyCondition"}
::: py-attribute-table-column
Attributes

- [conditions](#flogin.conditions.AnyCondition.conditions){.reference
  .internal}
:::
::::

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.conditions.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[AnyCondition]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[conditions]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/conditions.html#AnyCondition){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.conditions.AnyCondition "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This builtin search condition acts similiarly to the builtin
  [`any`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} function. It only
  returns [`True`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} if any one of
  the given conditions return [`True`{.docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}.

  This condition will set [[`flogin.query.Query.condition_data`{.xref
  .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.query.Query.condition_data "flogin.query.Query.condition_data"){.reference
  .internal} to a tuple containing two values. The first value will be
  the condition that returned true, and the second will be the condition
  data that the condition gave.

  :::: {.highlight-default .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      (condition, query.condition_data)
  :::
  ::::

  [[conditions]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.conditions.AnyCondition.conditions "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : A list that contains all the conditions that should be used with
    this condition.

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : list\[[[condition]{.std
      .std-ref}](search_handlers.html#condition-example){.reference
      .internal}\]

: This class is a wrapper around Flow's API to make it easy to make
  requests and receive results.

  ::: {.admonition .note}
  Note

  Do not initialize this class yourself, instead use [[`Plugin`{.xref
  .py .py-class .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin "flogin.plugin.Plugin"){.reference
  .internal}'s [[`api`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.api "flogin.plugin.Plugin.api"){.reference
  .internal} attribute to get an instance.
  :::

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[update_results]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[raw_query]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[results]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/flow/api.html#FlowLauncherAPI.update_results){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI.update_results "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    Tells flow to change the results shown to the user

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    The [`raw_query`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} parameter
    is required by flow launcher, and must be the same as the current
    raw query in flow launcher for the results to successfully update.
    :::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.testing.plugin_tester.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[PluginTester]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[\[]{.sig-paren}*[[PluginT]{.pre}]{.n}*[\]]{.sig-paren}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[plugin]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[metadata]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[flow_api_client]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[flow_version]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[flow_application_dir]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[flow_program_dir]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/testing/plugin_tester.html#PluginTester){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This can be used to write tests for your plugins.

  See the [[Testing your plugin]{.doc}](testing.html){.reference
  .internal} guide for more information on writing tests.

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : - **plugin** ([[`Plugin`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin "flogin.plugin.Plugin"){.reference
      .internal}) -- Your plugin

    - **metadata** ([[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
      .internal} \| dict\[str, Any\] \| None) -- Your plugin's metadata.
      If [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} is passed,
      flogin will attempt to get the metadata from your
      [`plugin.json`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} file. The
      [[`PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata()`{.xref .py .py-func
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata "flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata"){.reference
      .internal} and
      [[`PluginTester.create_bogus_plugin_metadata()`{.xref .py .py-func
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_bogus_plugin_metadata "flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_bogus_plugin_metadata"){.reference
      .internal} classmethods have been provided for creating
      [[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
      .internal} objects.

    - **flow_api_client** (*Optional\[Any\]*) -- If not passed, flogin
      will use a filler class which will raise a runtime error whenever
      an attribute is accessed. If passed, you should be passing an
      instance of a class which will replace [[`FlowLauncherAPI`{.xref
      .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI "flogin.flow.api.FlowLauncherAPI"){.reference
      .internal}, so make sure to implement the methods you need and
      handle them accordingly.

    - **flow_version** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) --

      This is an optional positional keyword that if set, will
      automatically set the enviroment variable
      [`FLOW_VERSION`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} to the
      value. This is useful if your code uses the [[`flow_version`{.xref
      .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.flow_version "flogin.plugin.Plugin.flow_version"){.reference
      .internal} property.

    - **flow_application_dir** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} \| [[`Path`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/pathlib.html#pathlib.Path "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) --

      This is an optional positional keyword that if set, will
      automatically set the enviroment variable
      [`FLOW_APPLICATION_DIRECTORY`{.docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre} to the value. This is useful if your code
      uses the [[`flow_application_dir`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.flow_application_dir "flogin.plugin.Plugin.flow_application_dir"){.reference
      .internal} property.

    - **flow_program_dir** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} \| [[`Path`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/pathlib.html#pathlib.Path "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) --

      This is an optional positional keyword that if set, will
      automatically set the enviroment variable
      [`FLOW_PROGRAM_DIRECTORY`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}
      to the value. This is useful if your code uses the
      [[`flow_program_dir`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin.flow_program_dir "flogin.plugin.Plugin.flow_program_dir"){.reference
      .internal} property.

  [[plugin]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.plugin "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Your plugin

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : [[`Plugin`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.plugin.Plugin "flogin.plugin.Plugin"){.reference
      .internal}

  [[classmethod]{.pre} ]{.property}[[create_bogus_plugin_metadata]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/testing/plugin_tester.html#PluginTester.create_bogus_plugin_metadata){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_bogus_plugin_metadata "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This classmethod can be used to easily and quickly create a
    [[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
    .internal} object that can be used for testing.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    Since the information that this classmethod generates is bogus, it
    is not recommended to use this when your plugin relies on the plugin
    metadata. Consider using
    [[`PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata()`{.xref .py .py-func
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata "flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata"){.reference
    .internal} instead.
    :::

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
      .internal} -- The [[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
      .internal} instance with your bogus information.

  [[classmethod]{.pre} ]{.property}[[create_plugin_metadata]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[id]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[name]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[author]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[version]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[description]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[website]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[disabled]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[False]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[directory]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[keywords]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[main_keyword]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[icon_path]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/testing/plugin_tester.html#PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.create_plugin_metadata "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This classmethod can be used to easily create a valid
    [[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
    .internal} object that has correct data.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : - **id** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The plugin's id

      - **name** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The plugin's name

      - **author** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The plugin's author

      - **version** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The plugin's version

      - **description** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The plugin's description

      - **website** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- The plugin's website. If not given, the
        following fstring will be used instead:
        [`f"https://github.com/{author}/{name}"`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}

      - **disabled** (Optional\[[[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- Whether or not to mark the plugin as disabled.
        Defaults to [`False`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}

      - **directory** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- The plugin's directory. Defaults to the current
        working directory.

      - **keywords** (Optional\[list\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class
        .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]\]) -- The plugin's keywords. Defaults to
        [`["*"]`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}

      - **main_keyword** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class
        .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- The plugin's main keyword. Defaults to the
        first keyword in the keywords parameter

      - **icon_path** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- The plugin's icon. Defaults to an invalid icon
        path.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`PluginMetadata`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata "flogin.flow.plugin_metadata.PluginMetadata"){.reference
      .internal} -- Your new metadata class.

  [[await]{.pre} ]{.property}[[test_context_menu]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[result]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[bypass_registration]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[False]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/testing/plugin_tester.html#PluginTester.test_context_menu){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.testing.plugin_tester.PluginTester.test_context_menu "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : This function is a
    [*coroutine*](https://docs.python.org/3/library/asyncio-task.html#coroutine){.reference
    .external}.

    This coroutine can be used to send a result's context menu, and get
    the response.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    You should use this to test your context menus instead of invoking
    them directly because this method implements the post-processing
    that flogin puts onto your context menu and query methods.
    :::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : - **result** ([[`Result`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result "flogin.jsonrpc.results.Result"){.reference
        .internal}) -- The result you want to invoke the context menu
        for.

      - **bypass_registration** ([[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- Whether or not to bypass the [`Result`{.docutils
        .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}[`has`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}[`not`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}[`been`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}[`registered`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre} error.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : [**ValueError**](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#ValueError "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} -- This will be raised when
      [`bypass_registration`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} is
      set to [`False`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}, and the
      given result has not been registered.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`QueryResponse`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.jsonrpc.responses.QueryResponse "flogin.jsonrpc.responses.QueryResponse"){.reference
      .internal} -- The query response object that would normally be
      sent to flow.

      - **settings** (Optional\[[[`Settings`{.xref .py .py-class
        .docutils .literal

[[flogin.utils.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[setup_logging]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[formatter]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[handler]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[logger]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/utils.html#setup_logging){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.utils.setup_logging "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: Sets up flogin's default logger.

  ::: versionchanged
  [Changed in version 2.0.0: ]{.versionmodified
  .changed}[[`setup_logging()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.utils.setup_logging "flogin.utils.setup_logging"){.reference
  .internal} now returns tuple\[[[`logging.Logger`{.xref .py .py-class
  .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Logger "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
  .external}, [[`logging.Handler`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Handler "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
  .external}\]
  :::

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : - **formatter** (Optional\[[[`logging.Formatter`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Formatter "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- The formatter to use, incase you don't want to
      use the default file formatter.

    - **handler** (Optional\[[[`logging.Handler`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Handler "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) --

      The handler object that should be added to the logger. Defaults to
      [[`logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.handlers.html#logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} with the following arguments:

      :::: {.highlight-py3 .notranslate}
      ::: highlight
          filename="flogin.log", maxBytes=1000000, encoding="UTF-8", backupCount=1
      :::
      ::::

      ::: versionadded
      [Added in version 2.0.0.]{.versionmodified .added}
      :::

    - **logger** (Optional\[[[`logging.Logger`{.xref .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Logger "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) --

      The logger object that the handler/formatter should be added to.

      ::: versionadded
      [Added in version 2.0.0.]{.versionmodified .added}
      :::

  Returns[:]{.colon}

  : tuple\[[[`logging.Logger`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Logger "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external}, [[`logging.Handler`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#logging.Handler "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external}\] -- The logger and handler used to setup the logs.

[[flogin.utils.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[print]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[values]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[sep]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[name]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/utils.html#print){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.utils.print "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A function that acts similar to the [builtin print
  function](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#print){.reference
  .external}, but uses the
  [logging](https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.html#module-logging){.reference
  .external} module instead.

  This helper function is provided to easily "print" text without having
  to setup a logging object, because the builtin print function does not
  work as expected due to the jsonrpc pipes.

  ::: versionadded
  [Added in version 1.1.0.]{.versionmodified .added}
  :::

  ::: versionchanged
  [Changed in version 2.0.0: ]{.versionmodified .changed}The default log
  name now defaults to the filepath of the file that called the function
  opposed to [`printing`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}.
  :::

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : - **\*values** ([[`object`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#object "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}) -- A list of values to print

    - **sep** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) -- The character that is used as the seperator
      between the values. Defaults to a space.

    - **name** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\]) --

      The name of the logger. Defaults to the filepath of the file the
      function is called from.

      ::: versionadded
      [Added in version 2.0.0.]{.versionmodified .added}
      :::

<!-- -->

[[[class]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[flogin.pip.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[Pip]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[libs_dir]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\'lib\']{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This is a helper class for dealing with pip in a production
  environment.

  When flow launcher installs python, it does not install pip. Because
  of that, this class will temp-install pip while you need it, then
  delete it when you're done with it.

  ::: versionadded
  [Added in version 2.0.0.]{.versionmodified .added}
  :::

  ::: {.admonition .warning}
  Warning

  This class is blocking, and should only be used before you load your
  plugin.
  :::

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : **libs_dir** (Optional\[[[`pathlib.Path`{.xref .py .py-class
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/pathlib.html#pathlib.Path "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external} \| [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external}\]) -- The directory that your plugin's dependencies are
    installed to. Defaults to [`lib`{.docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}.

  Example

  This should be used before your plugin gets loaded. Here is an example
  of what your main file might look like when using pip:

  :::: {.highlight-py3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      # Add your paths
      sys.path.append(...)
      sys.path.append(...)

      from flogin import Pip

      with Pip() as pip:
          pip.ensure_installed("msgspec") # ensure the msgspec package is installed correctly

      # import and run your plugin
      from plugin.plugin import YourPlugin
      YourPlugin().run()
  :::
  ::::

  ::: {.docutils .container}

  [[with]{.pre} [Pip(\...)]{.pre} [as]{.pre} [pip:]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}

  : [[`Pip`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip "flogin.pip.Pip"){.reference
    .internal} can be used as a context manager, with
    [[`Pip.download_pip()`{.xref .py .py-meth .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip "flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip"){.reference
    .internal} being called on enter and [[`Pip.delete_pip()`{.xref .py
    .py-meth .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip.delete_pip "flogin.pip.Pip.delete_pip"){.reference
    .internal} being called on exit.
  :::

  [[delete_pip]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip.delete_pip){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.delete_pip "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Deletes the temp version of pip installed on the system.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    This is automatically called when using [[`Pip`{.xref .py .py-class
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip "flogin.pip.Pip"){.reference
    .internal} as a context manager.
    :::

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}

  [[download_pip]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip.download_pip){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Downloads the temp version of pip from pypa.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    This is automatically called when using [[`Pip`{.xref .py .py-class
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip "flogin.pip.Pip"){.reference
    .internal} as a context manager.
    :::

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : [**UnableToDownloadPip**](#flogin.errors.UnableToDownloadPip "flogin.errors.UnableToDownloadPip"){.reference
      .internal} -- This is raised when an error occured while
      attempting to download pip.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}

  [[ensure_installed]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[package]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[[\*]{.pre}]{.abbr title="Keyword-only parameters separator (PEP 3102)"}]{.keyword-only-separator .o}*, *[[module]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip.ensure_installed){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.ensure_installed "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Ensures a package is properly installed, and if not, reinstalls it.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : - **package** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}) -- The name of the package on PyPi that you want to
        install.

      - **module** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external}\]) -- The name of the module you want to check to see
        if its installed. Defaults to the [`package`{.docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre} value.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : [**PipException**](#flogin.errors.PipException "flogin.errors.PipException"){.reference
      .internal} -- This is raised when the returncode that pip gives
      indicates an error.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`bool`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} -- [`True`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}
      indicates that the package wasn't properly installed, and was
      successfully reinstalled. [`False`{.docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre} indicates that the package was already
      properly installed.

  [[freeze]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip.freeze){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.freeze "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Returns a list of installed packages from [`pip`{.docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.docutils .literal
    .notranslate}[`freeze`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    The directory checked for packages is set in [[`Pip.libs_dir`{.xref
    .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip.libs_dir "flogin.pip.Pip.libs_dir"){.reference
    .internal}.
    :::

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : [**PipException**](#flogin.errors.PipException "flogin.errors.PipException"){.reference
      .internal} -- This is raised when the returncode that pip gives
      indicates an error.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : list\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}\] -- The list of packages and versions.

  [[install_packages]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[packages]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip.install_packages){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.install_packages "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : An easy way to install packages for your plugin.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    The packages will be installed to the directory set in
    [[`Pip.libs_dir`{.xref .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip.libs_dir "flogin.pip.Pip.libs_dir"){.reference
    .internal}.
    :::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : **\*packages** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}) -- The name of the packages on PyPi that you want to
      install.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : [**PipException**](#flogin.errors.PipException "flogin.errors.PipException"){.reference
      .internal} -- This is raised when the returncode that pip gives
      indicates an error.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}

  [[[property]{.pre}]{.k}[ ]{.w}]{.property}[[libs_dir]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.libs_dir "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : The directory that your plugin's dependencies are installed to.

    Type[:]{.colon}

    : [[`pathlib.Path`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/pathlib.html#pathlib.Path "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}

  [[run]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[\*]{.pre}]{.o}[[args]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/pip.html#Pip.run){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.pip.Pip.run "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

  : Runs a pip CLI command.

    This method is used to interact directly with pip.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    This method can not be used until [[`download_pip()`{.xref .py
    .py-meth .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip "flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip"){.reference
    .internal} is ran, which you can do by calling it manually or using
    [[`Pip`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip "flogin.pip.Pip"){.reference
    .internal} as a context manager.
    :::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    : **\*args** ([[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external}) -- The args that should be passed to pip. Ex:
      [`help`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    : - [**PipExecutionError**](#flogin.errors.PipExecutionError "flogin.errors.PipExecutionError"){.reference
        .internal} -- This is raised when the returncode that pip gives
        indicates an error.

      - [**RuntimeError**](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#RuntimeError "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
        .external} -- This is raised when [[`Pip.download_pip()`{.xref
        .py .py-meth .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip "flogin.pip.Pip.download_pip"){.reference
        .internal} has not ran yet.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    : [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
      .external} -- The output from pip.
