::: {#caching .section}
[]{#caching-reference}

## Caching[¶](#caching "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

[[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[flogin.caching.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[cached_property]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[obj]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/caching.html#cached_property){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.caching.cached_property "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A decorator that is similar to the builtin
  [functools.cached_property](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functools.html#functools.cached_property){.reference
  .external} decorator, but is async-safe and implements the ability to
  use [[`clear_cache()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.caching.clear_cache "flogin.caching.clear_cache"){.reference
  .internal}.

  This decorator can also be called with the optional positional
  argument acting as a [`name`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}
  argument. This is useful when using [[`clear_cache()`{.xref .py
  .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.caching.clear_cache "flogin.caching.clear_cache"){.reference
  .internal} as it lets you choose which items you want to clear the
  cache of.

  Example

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      class X:
          @cached_property
          def test(self):
              ...
  :::
  ::::

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      class X:
          @cached_property("test_prop")
          def test(self):
              ...
  :::
  ::::

<!-- -->

[[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[flogin.caching.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[cached_coro]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[obj]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/caching.html#cached_coro){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.caching.cached_coro "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A decorator to cache a coroutine's contents based on the passed
  arguments. This decorator can also be called with the optional
  positional argument acting as a [`name`{.docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre} argument. This is useful when using
  [[`clear_cache()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.caching.clear_cache "flogin.caching.clear_cache"){.reference
  .internal} as it lets you choose which items you want to clear the
  cache of.

  ::: {.admonition .note}
  Note

  The arguments passed to the coroutine must be hashable.
  :::

  Example

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      @plugin.search()
      @cached_coro
      async def handler(query):
          ...
  :::
  ::::

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      @plugin.search()
      @cached_coro("search-handler")
      async def handler(query):
          ...
  :::
  ::::

<!-- -->

[[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[flogin.caching.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[cached_gen]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[obj]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/caching.html#cached_gen){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.caching.cached_gen "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A decorator to cache the contents of an async generator based on the
  passed arguments. This decorator can also be called with the optional
  positional argument acting as a [`name`{.docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre} argument. This is useful when using
  [[`clear_cache()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.caching.clear_cache "flogin.caching.clear_cache"){.reference
  .internal} as it lets you choose which items you want to clear the
  cache of.

  ::: {.admonition .note}
  Note

  The arguments passed to the generator must be hashable.
  :::

  Example

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      @plugin.search()
      .cached_gen
      async def handler(query):
          ...
  :::
  ::::

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      @plugin.search()
      @cached_gen("search-handler")
      async def handler(query):
          ...
  :::
  ::::

<!-- -->

[[@]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[flogin.caching.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[cached_callable]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[obj]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/caching.html#cached_callable){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.caching.cached_callable "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: A decorator to cache a callable's output based on the passed
  arguments. This decorator can also be called with the optional
  positional argument acting as a [`name`{.docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre} argument. This is useful when using
  [[`clear_cache()`{.xref .py .py-func .docutils .literal
  .notranslate}]{.pre}](#flogin.caching.clear_cache "flogin.caching.clear_cache"){.reference
  .internal} as it lets you choose which items you want to clear the
  cache of.

  ::: {.admonition .note}
  Note

  The arguments passed to the callable must be hashable.
  :::

  Example

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      @cached_callable
      def foo(bar):
          ...
  :::
  ::::

  :::: {.highlight-python3 .notranslate}
  ::: highlight
      @cached_callable("search-handler")
      def foo(bar):
          ...
  :::
  ::::

<!-- -->

[[flogin.caching.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[clear_cache]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[key]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[\...]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](_modules/flogin/caching.html#clear_cache){.reference .internal}[¶](#flogin.caching.clear_cache "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

: This function is used to clear the cache of items that have been
  cached with this module.

  The caching decorators provide an optional positional argument that
  acts as a [`name`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} argument,
  which is used in combination of this function.

  Parameters[:]{.colon}

  : **key** (Optional\[[[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external} \| [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}\]) --
    If [[`str`{.xref .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#str "(in Python v3.14)"){.reference
    .external} is passed, every cached item with a name equal to
    [`key`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} will have their cache
    cleared. If [`None`{.docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} is
    passed, every cached item with a name equal to [`None`{.docutils
    .literal .notranslate}]{.pre} will have their cache cleared (default
    value for a cached item's name is [`None`{.docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}). Lastly, if the [`key`{.docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre} parameter is not passed at all, all caches will
    be cleared.
:::
